U &= \frac{nO+mC}{m+n} \\
&= \frac{o+4C}{4+1} \\
&= \frac{0+4\vec{c}}{5} \\
&= \frac{4}{5}\vec{c}
\end{aligned}$$

09373d18025f1daf2d92dca44f19af4ea4806b9a: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
V &= \frac{nC+mD}{m+n} \\
&= \frac{2C+3D}{3+2} \\

&= \frac{2}{5}\vec{c}+\frac{3}{5}\vec{d}
\end{aligned}$$

\end{aligned}$$
